hydroaj.hydraulics.bazin_chezy_c(hydraulic_radius: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], gamma: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray]) -> Union[float, numpy.ndarray]

Computes bazin chezy c.

SI Units: hydraulic_radius [m], gamma [SI units] -> returns [dimensionless]
Equation: C = 87.0 / (1.0 + gamma / sqrt(R_h))
Parameter Units & Details (3 params)
  • hydraulic_radius : Hydraulic radius R_h = A / P [unit = m]
  • gamma : Input parameter [unit = SI units]
  • Output : Runoff / discharge coefficient [unit = dimensionless]

hydroaj.hydraulics.belanger_conjugate_depth(initial_depth: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], froude_1: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray]) -> Union[float, numpy.ndarray]

Computes belanger conjugate depth.

SI Units: initial_depth [m], froude_1 [dimensionless] -> returns [m]
Equation: y_2 = (y_1 / 2) * (sqrt(1 + 8 * Fr_1^2) - 1)
Parameter Units & Details (3 params)
  • initial_depth : Supercritical initial depth y_1 [unit = m]
  • froude_1 : Supercritical inflow Froude number Fr_1 [unit = dimensionless]
  • Output : Hydraulic head / depth [unit = m]

hydroaj.hydraulics.blasius_friction_factor(reynolds: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray]) -> Union[float, numpy.ndarray]

Computes Darcy friction factor for smooth pipes in turbulent flow (Re < 10^5) using Blasius' formula:

SI Units: reynolds [dimensionless] -> returns [standard SI units]
Equation: f = 0.3164 / Re^0.25
Parameter Units & Details (2 params)
  • reynolds : Reynolds number Re = V * D / nu [unit = dimensionless]
  • Output : Computed numerical result [unit = standard SI units]

hydroaj.hydraulics.cavitation_index(pressure_abs: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], vapor_pressure: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], velocity: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], density: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray] = 1000.0) -> Union[float, numpy.ndarray]

Computes Thoma cavitation index sigma = (P_abs - P_v) / (0.5 * rho * V^2).

SI Units: pressure_abs [Pa (N/m^2)], vapor_pressure [Pa (N/m^2)], velocity [m/s], density [kg/m^3 (~1000.0)] -> returns [standard SI units]
Equation: Computes Thoma cavitation index sigma = (P_abs - P_v) / (0.5 * rho * V^2).
Parameter Units & Details (5 params)
  • pressure_abs : Absolute static fluid pressure P_abs [unit = Pa (N/m^2)]
  • vapor_pressure : Water vapor pressure P_v [unit = Pa (N/m^2)]
  • velocity : Mean cross-sectional flow velocity V [unit = m/s]
  • density : Fluid mass density rho [unit = kg/m^3 (~1000.0)]
  • Output : Computed numerical result [unit = standard SI units]

hydroaj.hydraulics.colebrook_white(relative_roughness: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], reynolds: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray]) -> Union[float, numpy.ndarray]

Vectorized iterative root-finding for Colebrook-White friction factor f:

SI Units: relative_roughness [SI units], reynolds [dimensionless] -> returns [standard SI units]
Equation: 1 / sqrt(f) = -2 * log10((eps/D)/3.7 + 2.51/(Re * sqrt(f)))
Parameter Units & Details (3 params)
  • relative_roughness : Input parameter [unit = SI units]
  • reynolds : Reynolds number Re = V * D / nu [unit = dimensionless]
  • Output : Computed numerical result [unit = standard SI units]

SI Units: roughness [s/m^(1/3)], critical_r_h [SI units] -> returns [m/m]
Equation: S_c = (g * n^2) / (k^2 * R_hc^(4/3))

  • system : Unit system ('si' for standard metric units, 'us' for US customary units) [unit = default='si']
  • Output : Longitudinal bed slope [unit = m/m]

Computes culvert inlet submerged.

SI Units: discharge [m^3/s], diameter [m], area [m^2], slope [m/m (decimal)], c_coeff [s^2 / m^5], y_const [m] -> returns [standard SI units]
Equation: HW_d = c * (Q / (A * sqrt(D)))^2 + Y - 0.5 * S
Parameter Units & Details (7 params)

  • diameter : Internal conduit / pipe diameter D [unit = m]
  • area : Cross-sectional flow area A [unit = m^2]
  • slope : Longitudinal bed slope / hydraulic gradient S_0 [unit = m/m (decimal)]
  • c_coeff : Well turbulent screen loss coefficient C [unit = s^2 / m^5]
  • y_const : Flow depth [unit = m]

Equation: HW_d = (H_c / D) + K * (Q / (A * sqrt(D)))^M - 0.5 * S

hydroaj.hydraulics.culvert_outlet_control_head(tailwater_term: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], total_losses: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], length: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], slope: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray]) -> Union[float, numpy.ndarray]

Computes culvert outlet control head.

SI Units: tailwater_term [SI units], total_losses [SI units], length [m], slope [m/m (decimal)] -> returns [m]
Equation: HW_o = h_o + H - L * S_0; H = (1 + K_e + (29 * n^2 * L) / (R_h^(4/3))) * (V^2 / (2 * g))
Parameter Units & Details (5 params)
  • tailwater_term : Input parameter [unit = SI units]
  • total_losses : Input parameter [unit = SI units]
  • length : Reach / conduit / flow path length L [unit = m]
  • slope : Longitudinal bed slope / hydraulic gradient S_0 [unit = m/m (decimal)]
  • Output : Hydraulic head / depth [unit = m]

hydroaj.hydraulics.darcy_weisbach_q(head_loss: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], friction_factor: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], length: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], diameter: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], system: Literal['si', 'us'] = 'si') -> Union[float, numpy.ndarray]

Computes darcy weisbach q.

SI Units: head_loss [m], friction_factor [dimensionless], length [m], diameter [m] -> returns [m^3/s]
Equation: Q = (pi * D^2 / 4) * sqrt((2 * g * D * h_f) / (f * L))

  • head_loss : Energy friction head loss h_f [unit = m]
  • friction_factor : Darcy-Weisbach friction factor f [unit = dimensionless]
  • length : Reach / conduit / flow path length L [unit = m]
  • diameter : Internal conduit / pipe diameter D [unit = m]
  • system : Unit system ('si' for standard metric units, 'us' for US customary units) [unit = default='si']
  • Output : Volumetric flow discharge [unit = m^3/s]

Computes froude fr.

hydroaj.hydraulics.geom_circular(diameter: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], depth: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray]) -> Tuple[Union[float, numpy.ndarray], ...]

Computes geom circular.

SI Units: diameter [m], depth [m] -> returns [m]
Equation: theta = 2*acos(1 - 2*y/D); A = (D^2 / 8)*(theta - sin(theta)); P = D*theta/2; R_h = A / P; T = D*sin(theta/2); D_h = A / T
Parameter Units & Details (3 params)
  • diameter : Internal conduit / pipe diameter D [unit = m]
  • depth : Water flow depth y [unit = m]
  • Output : Geometric properties tuple (Area [unit = m]
Hydraulics

hydroaj.hydraulics.geom_parabolic(top_width: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], depth: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray]) -> Tuple[Union[float, numpy.ndarray], ...]

Computes geom parabolic.

SI Units: top_width [m], depth [m] -> returns [m]
Equation: A = (2/3)*T*y; P = T + (8/3)*(y^2 / T); R_h = A / P; D_h = (2/3)*y; y_bar = (2/5)*y
Parameter Units & Details (3 params)
  • top_width : Channel free-surface top width T [unit = m]
  • depth : Water flow depth y [unit = m]
  • Output : Geometric properties tuple (Area [unit = m]
Hydraulics

hydroaj.hydraulics.geom_rectangular(bottom_width: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], depth: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray]) -> Tuple[Union[float, numpy.ndarray], ...]

Computes geom rectangular.

SI Units: bottom_width [m], depth [m] -> returns [m]
Equation: A = b * y; P = b + 2*y; R_h = A / P; T = b; D_h = y; y_bar = y / 2
Parameter Units & Details (3 params)
  • bottom_width : Channel base width b [unit = m]
  • depth : Water flow depth y [unit = m]
  • Output : Geometric properties tuple (Area [unit = m]
Hydraulics

hydroaj.hydraulics.geom_trapezoidal(bottom_width: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], side_slope: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], depth: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray]) -> Tuple[Union[float, numpy.ndarray], ...]

Computes geom trapezoidal.

SI Units: bottom_width [m], side_slope [dimensionless], depth [m] -> returns [m]
Equation: A = (b + m*y)*y; P = b + 2*y*sqrt(1 + m^2); R_h = A / P; T = b + 2*m*y; D_h = A / T; y_bar = y*(2*b + m*y)/(3*(b + m*y))
Parameter Units & Details (4 params)
  • bottom_width : Channel base width b [unit = m]
  • side_slope : Side slope ratio m (1V : mH) [unit = dimensionless]
  • depth : Water flow depth y [unit = m]
  • Output : Geometric properties tuple (Area [unit = m]
Hydraulics

hydroaj.hydraulics.geom_triangular(side_slope: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], depth: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray]) -> Tuple[Union[float, numpy.ndarray], ...]

Computes geom triangular.

SI Units: side_slope [dimensionless], depth [m] -> returns [m]
Equation: A = m * y^2; P = 2*y*sqrt(1 + m^2); R_h = A / P; T = 2*m*y; D_h = y / 2; y_bar = y / 3
Parameter Units & Details (3 params)
  • side_slope : Side slope ratio m (1V : mH) [unit = dimensionless]
  • depth : Water flow depth y [unit = m]
  • Output : Geometric properties tuple (Area [unit = m]

hydroaj.hydraulics.hardy_cross_correction(sum_head_loss: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], sum_derivative: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], n_exp: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray] = 2.0) -> Union[float, numpy.ndarray]

Computes hardy cross correction.

SI Units: sum_head_loss [m], sum_derivative [SI units], n_exp [SI units] -> returns [standard SI units]
Equation: delta_Q = -sum(r * Q * abs(Q)^(n - 1)) / (n * sum(r * abs(Q)^(n - 1)))

  • n_exp : Input parameter [unit = SI units]

hydroaj.hydraulics.hazen_williams(length: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], discharge: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], c_coefficient: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], diameter: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], system: Literal['si', 'us'] = 'si') -> Union[float, numpy.ndarray]

Hazen-Williams head loss:

SI Units: length [m], discharge [m^3/s], c_coefficient [dimensionless], diameter [m] -> returns [standard SI units]
Equation: h_f = (k_hw * L * Q^1.852) / (C^1.852 * D^4.87)
Parameter Units & Details (6 params)

  • discharge : Volumetric flow rate / discharge Q [unit = m^3/s]
  • c_coefficient : Input parameter [unit = dimensionless]
  • diameter : Internal conduit / pipe diameter D [unit = m]
  • system : Unit system ('si' for standard metric units, 'us' for US customary units) [unit = default='si']
  • Output : Computed numerical result [unit = standard SI units]

hydroaj.hydraulics.hazen_williams_c(head_loss: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], discharge: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], diameter: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], length: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], system: Literal['si', 'us'] = 'si') -> Union[float, numpy.ndarray]

Inversion: Hazen-Williams C = ((k_L * L * Q^1.852) / (h_f * D^4.8704))^(1 / 1.852)

Equation: Inversion: Hazen-Williams C = ((k_L * L * Q^1.852) / (h_f * D^4.8704))^(1 / 1.852)
Parameter Units & Details (6 params)
  • head_loss : Energy friction head loss h_f [unit = m]
  • discharge : Volumetric flow rate / discharge Q [unit = m^3/s]
  • diameter : Internal conduit / pipe diameter D [unit = m]
  • length : Reach / conduit / flow path length L [unit = m]
  • system : Unit system ('si' for standard metric units, 'us' for US customary units) [unit = default='si']
  • Output : Runoff / discharge coefficient [unit = dimensionless]

hydroaj.hydraulics.hazen_williams_d(head_loss: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], discharge: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], roughness_c: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], length: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], system: Literal['si', 'us'] = 'si') -> Union[float, numpy.ndarray]

Equation: D = ((k_hw * L * Q^1.852) / (h_f * C^1.852))^(1 / 4.87)

  • discharge : Volumetric flow rate / discharge Q [unit = m^3/s]

  • length : Reach / conduit / flow path length L [unit = m]
  • system : Unit system ('si' for standard metric units, 'us' for US customary units) [unit = default='si']
  • Output : Diameter [unit = mm if 'd50' in fn else 'm']

  • discharge : Volumetric flow rate / discharge Q [unit = m^3/s]

hydroaj.hydraulics.hazen_williams_l(head_loss: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], discharge: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], roughness_c: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], diameter: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], system: Literal['si', 'us'] = 'si') -> Union[float, numpy.ndarray]

Inversion: Pipe length L = (h_f * C^1.852 * D^4.8704) / (k_L * Q^1.852)

SI Units: head_loss [m], discharge [m^3/s], roughness_c [dimensionless (typically 80 - 150)], diameter [m] -> returns [km if 'km' in fn else 'm']
Equation: Inversion: Pipe length L = (h_f * C^1.852 * D^4.8704) / (k_L * Q^1.852)
Parameter Units & Details (6 params)
  • head_loss : Energy friction head loss h_f [unit = m]
  • discharge : Volumetric flow rate / discharge Q [unit = m^3/s]
  • roughness_c : Hazen-Williams roughness coefficient C [unit = dimensionless (typically 80 - 150)]
  • diameter : Internal conduit / pipe diameter D [unit = m]
  • system : Unit system ('si' for standard metric units, 'us' for US customary units) [unit = default='si']
  • Output : Length [unit = km if 'km' in fn else 'm']

hydroaj.hydraulics.hazen_williams_q(head_loss: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], roughness_c: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], diameter: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], length: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], system: Literal['si', 'us'] = 'si') -> Union[float, numpy.ndarray]

Computes hazen williams q.

SI Units: head_loss [m], roughness_c [dimensionless (typically 80 - 150)], diameter [m], length [m] -> returns [m^3/s]
Equation: Q = ((h_f * C^1.852 * D^4.87) / (k_hw * L))^(1 / 1.852)
Parameter Units & Details (6 params)
  • head_loss : Energy friction head loss h_f [unit = m]
  • roughness_c : Hazen-Williams roughness coefficient C [unit = dimensionless (typically 80 - 150)]
  • diameter : Internal conduit / pipe diameter D [unit = m]
  • length : Reach / conduit / flow path length L [unit = m]
  • system : Unit system ('si' for standard metric units, 'us' for US customary units) [unit = default='si']
  • Output : Volumetric flow discharge [unit = m^3/s]

hydroaj.hydraulics.hazen_williams_c(head_loss: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], discharge: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], diameter: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], length: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], system: Literal['si', 'us'] = 'si') -> Union[float, numpy.ndarray]

Inversion: Hazen-Williams C = ((k_L * L * Q^1.852) / (h_f * D^4.8704))^(1 / 1.852)

SI Units: head_loss [m], discharge [m^3/s], diameter [m], length [m] -> returns [dimensionless]
Equation: Inversion: Hazen-Williams C = ((k_L * L * Q^1.852) / (h_f * D^4.8704))^(1 / 1.852)
Parameter Units & Details (6 params)
  • head_loss : Energy friction head loss h_f [unit = m]
  • discharge : Volumetric flow rate / discharge Q [unit = m^3/s]
  • diameter : Internal conduit / pipe diameter D [unit = m]
  • length : Reach / conduit / flow path length L [unit = m]
  • system : Unit system ('si' for standard metric units, 'us' for US customary units) [unit = default='si']
  • Output : Runoff / discharge coefficient [unit = dimensionless]

hydroaj.hydraulics.hydraulic_jump_y1(depth_2: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], froude_1: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray]) -> Union[float, numpy.ndarray]

Computes hydraulic jump y1.

SI Units: depth_2 [m], froude_1 [dimensionless] -> returns [standard SI units]
Equation: y_1 = (y_2 / 2) * (sqrt(1 + 8 * Fr_2^2) - 1)
Parameter Units & Details (3 params)
  • depth_2 : Subcritical sequent flow depth y_2 [unit = m]
  • froude_1 : Supercritical inflow Froude number Fr_1 [unit = dimensionless]
  • Output : Computed numerical result [unit = standard SI units]
Hydraulics

hydroaj.hydraulics.hydraulic_jump_efficiency(froude_1: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray]) -> Union[float, numpy.ndarray]

Computes hydraulic jump efficiency.

SI Units: froude_1 [dimensionless] -> returns [standard SI units]
Equation: E_2_over_E_1 = ((1 + 8 * Fr_1^2)^(1.5) - 4 * Fr_1^2 + 1) / (8 * Fr_1^2 * (2 + Fr_1^2))
Parameter Units & Details (2 params)
  • froude_1 : Supercritical inflow Froude number Fr_1 [unit = dimensionless]
  • Output : Computed numerical result [unit = standard SI units]

SI Units: discharge [m^3/s], silt_factor [dimensionless] -> returns [m/m]
Equation: S = f^(5/3) / (3340 * Q^(1/6)) [dimensionless m/m]
Parameter Units & Details (3 params)
  • discharge : Volumetric flow rate / discharge Q [unit = m^3/s]
  • silt_factor : Lacey's silt factor f [unit = dimensionless]
  • Output : Longitudinal bed slope [unit = m/m]
Hydraulics

Computes Lacey's regime non-silting, non-scouring velocity V.

SI Units: discharge [m^3/s], silt_factor [dimensionless] -> returns [m/s]
Equation: V = ((Q * f^2) / 140)^(1/6) [Q in m^3/s, V in m/s]

  • silt_factor : Lacey's silt factor f [unit = dimensionless]

hydroaj.hydraulics.optimal_rectangular_section(depth: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray]) -> Tuple[Union[float, numpy.ndarray], ...]

Computes optimal rectangular section.

SI Units: depth [m] -> returns [standard SI units]
Equation: b = 2 * y; R_h = y / 2; A = 2 * y^2; P = 4 * y
Parameter Units & Details (2 params)
  • depth : Water flow depth y [unit = m]
  • Output : Computed numerical result [unit = standard SI units]
Hydraulics

hydroaj.hydraulics.optimal_trapezoidal_section(depth: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray]) -> Tuple[Union[float, numpy.ndarray], ...]

Computes optimal trapezoidal section.

SI Units: depth [m] -> returns [standard SI units]
Equation: m = 1 / sqrt(3); b = 2 * y * (sqrt(1 + m^2) - m); R_h = y / 2
Parameter Units & Details (2 params)
  • depth : Water flow depth y [unit = m]
  • Output : Computed numerical result [unit = standard SI units]

hydroaj.hydraulics.optimal_triangular_section(depth: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray]) -> Tuple[Union[float, numpy.ndarray], ...]

Computes optimal triangular section.

SI Units: depth [m] -> returns [standard SI units]
Equation: m = 1.0; R_h = y / (2 * sqrt(2)); A = y^2; P = 2 * sqrt(2) * y
Parameter Units & Details (2 params)

  • Output : Computed numerical result [unit = standard SI units]

Computes parallel pipes distribution.

SI Units: total_discharge [m^3/s], branch_conductance [SI units], sum_conductance [SI units] -> returns [standard SI units]
Equation: Q_i = Q_total * (sqrt(D_i^5 / L_i)) / sum(sqrt(D_j^5 / L_j))
Parameter Units & Details (4 params)

Computes pavlovskii composite n.

SI Units: perimeters [SI units], roughnesses [SI units] -> returns [s/m^(1/3) or dimensionless]
Equation: n_bar = (sum(P_i * n_i^2) / sum(P_i))^(1/2)

hydroaj.hydraulics.wave_celerity(bulk_modulus: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], density: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], elastic_modulus: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], diameter: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], wall_thickness: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray]) -> Union[float, numpy.ndarray]

Water hammer wave celerity: a = sqrt((K / rho) / (1 + (K / E) * (D / e)))

SI Units: bulk_modulus [SI units], density [kg/m^3 (~1000.0)], elastic_modulus [SI units], diameter [m], wall_thickness [SI units] -> returns [standard SI units]
Equation: Water hammer wave celerity: a = sqrt((K / rho) / (1 + (K / E) * (D / e)))
Parameter Units & Details (6 params)

  • density : Fluid mass density rho [unit = kg/m^3 (~1000.0)]
  • elastic_modulus : Input parameter [unit = SI units]

  • wall_thickness : Input parameter [unit = SI units]
  • Output : Computed numerical result [unit = standard SI units]

hydroaj.hydrology.fao56_penman_monteith(net_radiation: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], temp_c: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], wind_speed_2m: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], es: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], ea: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], delta_slope: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], psychrometric_gamma: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], soil_heat_flux: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray] = 0.0) -> Union[float, numpy.ndarray]

Computes daily reference evapotranspiration ET_0 using the international FAO-56 Penman-Monteith equation.

SI Units: net_radiation [MJ / m^2 / day], temp_c [deg C], wind_speed_2m [m/s], es [kPa], ea [kPa], delta_slope [kPa / deg C], psychrometric_gamma [kPa / deg C], soil_heat_flux [MJ / m^2 / day] -> returns [standard SI units]
Equation: ET_0 = [0.408 * Delta * (R_n - G) + gamma * (900 / (T + 273)) * u_2 * (e_s - e_a)] / [Delta + gamma * (1 + 0.34 * u_2)]
Parameter Units & Details (9 params)
  • net_radiation : Net radiation at the crop surface R_n [unit = MJ / m^2 / day]
  • temp_c : Mean daily air temperature at 2 m height T [unit = deg C]
  • wind_speed_2m : Mean daily wind speed at 2 m height u_2 [unit = m/s]
  • es : Mean saturation vapor pressure e_s [unit = kPa]
  • ea : Actual atmospheric vapor pressure e_a [unit = kPa]
  • delta_slope : Slope of saturation vapor pressure curve Delta [unit = kPa / deg C]

hydroaj.hydrology.muskingum_coefficients(travel_time_k: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], weighting_factor_x: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], time_step_dt: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray]) -> Tuple[Union[float, numpy.ndarray], ...]

Computes muskingum coefficients.

SI Units: travel_time_k [hours], weighting_factor_x [SI units], time_step_dt [hours] -> returns [standard SI units]
Equation: C_0 = (dt - 2*K*X)/(2*K*(1-X) + dt); C_1 = (dt + 2*K*X)/(2*K*(1-X) + dt); C_2 = (2*K*(1-X) - dt)/(2*K*(1-X) + dt)
Parameter Units & Details (4 params)
  • travel_time_k : Time parameter [unit = hours]
  • weighting_factor_x : Input parameter [unit = SI units]
  • time_step_dt : Time parameter [unit = hours]
  • Output : Computed numerical result [unit = standard SI units]

hydroaj.hydrology.priestley_taylor_et(net_radiation: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], temp_c: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], delta_slope: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], psychrometric_gamma: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], alpha: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray] = 1.26, soil_heat_flux: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray] = 0.0) -> Union[float, numpy.ndarray]

Computes radiation-driven potential evapotranspiration using the Priestley-Taylor equation (1972).

SI Units: net_radiation [MJ / m^2 / day], temp_c [deg C], delta_slope [kPa / deg C], psychrometric_gamma [kPa / deg C], alpha [dimensionless (~1.26)], soil_heat_flux [MJ / m^2 / day] -> returns [standard SI units]
Equation: ET = alpha * [Delta / (Delta + gamma)] * [(R_n - G) / lambda] [lambda = 2.45 MJ/kg]
Parameter Units & Details (7 params)
  • net_radiation : Net radiation at the crop surface R_n [unit = MJ / m^2 / day]
  • temp_c : Mean daily air temperature at 2 m height T [unit = deg C]
  • delta_slope : Slope of saturation vapor pressure curve Delta [unit = kPa / deg C]
  • psychrometric_gamma : Psychrometric constant gamma [unit = kPa / deg C]
  • alpha : Priestley-Taylor radiation coefficient alpha [unit = dimensionless (~1.26)]
  • soil_heat_flux : Soil heat flux density G [unit = MJ / m^2 / day]

Computes rational a.

hydroaj.groundwater.cooper_jacob_drawdown(pumping_rate: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], transmissivity: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], storativity: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], radius: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], time: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray]) -> Union[float, numpy.ndarray]

Computes cooper jacob drawdown.

Equation: s = (2.303 * Q / (4 * pi * T)) * log10((2.25 * T * t) / (r^2 * S))

hydroaj.groundwater.ghyben_herzberg_depth(freshwater_head: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], rho_f: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray] = 1000.0, rho_s: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray] = 1025.0) -> Union[float, numpy.ndarray]

Computes depth z to freshwater-saltwater interface below sea level via the Ghyben-Herzberg relation.

SI Units: freshwater_head [m], rho_f [kg/m^3 (~1000.0)], rho_s [kg/m^3 (~1025.0)] -> returns [m]
Equation: z = (rho_f / (rho_s - rho_f)) * h_f ~ 40.0 * h_f
Parameter Units & Details (4 params)
  • freshwater_head : Freshwater water table elevation above sea level h_f [unit = m]
  • rho_f : Density of freshwater rho_f [unit = kg/m^3 (~1000.0)]
  • rho_s : Density of saltwater rho_s [unit = kg/m^3 (~1025.0)]
  • Output : Hydraulic head / depth [unit = m]
Groundwater

hydroaj.groundwater.ghyben_herzberg_head(interface_depth: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], rho_f: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray] = 1000.0, rho_s: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray] = 1025.0) -> Union[float, numpy.ndarray]

Inversion: Solves for freshwater water table elevation h_f = ((rho_s - rho_f) / rho_f) * z.

SI Units: interface_depth [m], rho_f [kg/m^3 (~1000.0)], rho_s [kg/m^3 (~1025.0)] -> returns [m]
Equation: Inversion: Solves for freshwater water table elevation h_f = ((rho_s - rho_f) / rho_f) * z.
Parameter Units & Details (4 params)
  • interface_depth : Depth to freshwater-saltwater interface below sea level z [unit = m]
  • rho_f : Density of freshwater rho_f [unit = kg/m^3 (~1000.0)]
  • rho_s : Density of saltwater rho_s [unit = kg/m^3 (~1025.0)]
  • Output : Hydraulic head / depth [unit = m]

hydroaj.groundwater.theis_drawdown(pumping_rate: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], transmissivity: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], storativity: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], radius: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], time: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray]) -> Union[float, numpy.ndarray]

Computes theis drawdown.

SI Units: pumping_rate [SI units], transmissivity [m^2/s], storativity [dimensionless], radius [SI units], time [hours] -> returns [standard SI units]
Equation: s = (Q / (4 * pi * T)) * W(u); u = (r^2 * S) / (4 * T * t)

  • transmissivity : Aquifer transmissivity T [unit = m^2/s]
  • storativity : Aquifer storativity / storage coefficient S [unit = dimensionless]
  • radius : Input parameter [unit = SI units]
  • time : Elapsed infiltration or pumping time t [unit = hours]
  • Output : Computed numerical result [unit = standard SI units]

hydroaj.groundwater.thiem_confined(transmissivity: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], h1: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], h2: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], r1: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], r2: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray]) -> Union[float, numpy.ndarray]

Thiem steady-state pumping rate for confined aquifer:

SI Units: transmissivity [m^2/s], h1 [m], h2 [m], r1 [m], r2 [m] -> returns [standard SI units]
Equation: Q = (2 * pi * T * (h_2 - h_1)) / ln(r_2 / r_1)
Parameter Units & Details (6 params)
  • transmissivity : Aquifer transmissivity T [unit = m^2/s]
  • h1 : Hydraulic head at well 1 (h_1) [unit = m]
  • h2 : Hydraulic head at well 2 (h_2) [unit = m]
  • r1 : Distance to observation well 1 (r_1) [unit = m]
  • r2 : Distance to observation well 2 (r_2) [unit = m]
  • Output : Computed numerical result [unit = standard SI units]

  • radius_2 : Distance to far observation well r_2 [unit = m]

  • head_1 : Hydraulic head [unit = m]
  • head_2 : Hydraulic head [unit = m]

Thiem (Dupuit) steady-state pumping rate for unconfined aquifer:

SI Units: hydraulic_conductivity [m/s], h1 [m], h2 [m], r1 [m], r2 [m] -> returns [standard SI units]
Equation: Q = (pi * K * (h_2^2 - h_1^2)) / ln(r_2 / r_1)
Parameter Units & Details (6 params)
  • hydraulic_conductivity : Darcy saturated hydraulic conductivity K [unit = m/s]
  • h1 : Hydraulic head at well 1 (h_1) [unit = m]
  • h2 : Hydraulic head at well 2 (h_2) [unit = m]
  • r1 : Distance to observation well 1 (r_1) [unit = m]
  • r2 : Distance to observation well 2 (r_2) [unit = m]
  • Output : Computed numerical result [unit = standard SI units]

Computes discharge Q for a Sutro (proportional linear) weir.

SI Units: base_width [m], base_height [m], head [m], cd [dimensionless] -> returns [m^3/s]

SI Units: shields_param [SI units], specific_weight_sediment [SI units], specific_weight_water [SI units], grain_size_d50 [SI units] -> returns [standard SI units]
Equation: Inversion: Bed shear stress tau_0 = theta_* * (gamma_s - gamma_w) * d_50

  • specific_weight_sediment : Input parameter [unit = SI units]

Sediment

hydroaj.sediment.stokes_settling_d(settling_velocity: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], specific_gravity: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], kinematic_viscosity: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], system: Literal['si', 'us'] = 'si') -> Union[float, numpy.ndarray]

Inversion: Particle diameter d = sqrt((18 * nu * w_s) / (g * (S_s - 1)))

SI Units: settling_velocity [m/s], specific_gravity [SI units], kinematic_viscosity [SI units] -> returns [mm if 'd50' in fn else 'm']
Equation: Inversion: Particle diameter d = sqrt((18 * nu * w_s) / (g * (S_s - 1)))
Parameter Units & Details (5 params)
  • settling_velocity : Flow velocity [unit = m/s]
  • specific_gravity : Input parameter [unit = SI units]
  • kinematic_viscosity : Input parameter [unit = SI units]
  • system : Unit system ('si' for standard metric units, 'us' for US customary units) [unit = default='si']
  • Output : Diameter [unit = mm if 'd50' in fn else 'm']

hydroaj.sediment.stokes_settling_velocity(grain_diameter: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray], specific_gravity: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray] = 2.65, kinematic_viscosity: Union[float, int, Sequence[float], numpy.ndarray] = 1e-06, system: Literal['si', 'us'] = 'si') -> Union[float, numpy.ndarray]

Computes stokes settling velocity.

SI Units: grain_diameter [m], specific_gravity [SI units], kinematic_viscosity [SI units] -> returns [m/s]
Equation: w_s = (g * (S_s - 1) * d^2) / (18 * nu)
Parameter Units & Details (5 params)
  • grain_diameter : Pipe internal diameter [unit = m]
  • specific_gravity : Input parameter [unit = SI units]
  • kinematic_viscosity : Input parameter [unit = SI units]
  • system : Unit system ('si' for standard metric units, 'us' for US customary units) [unit = default='si']
  • Output : Flow velocity [unit = m/s]
